Fleetweft MVP evidence: PASS

Isolated Linux VM, four network namespaces (one controller and three nodes). This is not a hardware/three-host performance benchmark. Request timing includes new TLS connection setup, HTTP service time and full response read; driver/netns startup is excluded. Thirty sequential requests are a smoke measurement, not saturation throughput. RSS is reported separately for each service process and worker, not VM total memory. Orderly VM restart only. No power-loss or boot-zero-gap claim.
